c6185552724528eda466a99b9b611e0f62e5cefd: $$p(x;\theta)=\frac{q(x;\theta)}{Z(\theta)}$$

e81723c2e7ee6a9a65085dad28f344ef7b664ee8: $$\nabla_{x} \log\left(\frac{q(x)}{Z}\right)=\nabla_{x} \log(q(x)) - \nabla_x \log(Z) = \nabla_{x} \log(q(x))$$
